  1210: function findPlungerPumpDetailSlug(product: ProductSelectionProduct) {
  1211:   const candidates = [
  1212:     getSelectionLocalizedText(product.cardTitle, "en"),
  1213:     getSelectionLocalizedText(product.cardTitle, "zh"),
  1214:     product.productId,
  1215:     product.detailSlug,
  1216:   ]
  1217:     .map(normalizeModelKey)
  1218:     .filter(Boolean);
  1219: 

  1213:     getSelectionLocalizedText(product.cardTitle, "zh"),
  1214:     product.productId,
  1215:     product.detailSlug,
  1216:   ]
  1217:     .map(normalizeModelKey)
  1218:     .filter(Boolean);
  1219: 
  1220:   const matchedDetail = (plungerPumpDetails as any[]).find((detail) => {
  1221:     const detailCandidates = [
  1222:       detail.model,
  1223:       detail.productId,
  1224:       detail.slug,
  1225:       detail.detailSlug,
  1226:     ]
  1227:       .map(normalizeModelKey)
  1228:       .filter(Boolean);
  1229: 

  1234:     matchedDetail?.slug ||
  1235:       matchedDetail?.detailSlug ||
  1236:       product.detailSlug
  1237:   );
  1238: }
  1239: 
  1240: 
  1241: /* ===== FOREACH plunger pump model detail href helpers START ===== */
  1242: 
  1243: function cleanPlungerHrefText(value: unknown) {
  1244:   return String(value || "").trim();
  1245: }
  1246: 
  1247: function normalizePlungerPathPart(value: unknown) {
  1248:   const parts = cleanPlungerHrefText(value).split("/").filter(Boolean);
  1249:   return parts.length > 0 ? parts[parts.length - 1] : "";
  1250: }

  1253:   return cleanPlungerHrefText(value)
  1254:     .toLowerCase()
  1255:     .replace(/μ/g, "u")
  1256:     .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "-")
  1257:     .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, "");
  1258: }
  1259: 
  1260: function getPlungerPumpModelSlugForDetailHref(product: ProductSelectionProduct) {
  1261:   const existingSlug = normalizePlungerPathPart(product.detailSlug);
  1262: 
  1263:   if (/^(ea|sm|tm)-\d+-(pmma|peek)$/i.test(existingSlug)) {
  1264:     return existingSlug.toLowerCase();
  1265:   }
  1266: 
  1267:   const text = [
  1268:     getSelectionLocalizedText(product.cardTitle, "en"),
  1269:     getSelectionLocalizedText(product.cardTitle, "zh"),

  1301:       materialMatch[1].toLowerCase(),
  1302:     ].join("-");
  1303:   }
  1304: 
  1305:   return normalizePlungerModelSlug(getSelectionLocalizedText(product.cardTitle, "en") || getSelectionLocalizedText(product.cardTitle, "zh") || product.productId || product.detailSlug);
  1306: }

   217: function inferKind(data: DetailRecord, pathname = ""): ProductKind {
   218:   const text = [pathname, data.detailHref, data.href, data.selectionHref, data.productTypeId, data.productTypeName, data.categoryLabel, data.slug, data.title, data.name].filter(Boolean).join(" ").toLowerCase();
   219:   const rules: Array<[ProductKind, RegExp]> = [
   220:     ["hard-tube-fitting", /hard[- ]tube|硬管/], ["bulkhead-barbed-fitting", /bulkhead[- ]barbed|穿板倒刺/], ["thread-to-barbed-fitting", /thread[- ]to[- ]barb|thread to barb|螺纹转倒刺/], ["quick-connect-fitting", /quick[- ]connect|快插|快速接头/], ["luer-fitting", /luer|鲁尔/], ["female-thread-adapter", /female[- ]thread|内螺纹/], ["barbed-fitting", /barbed[- ]fitting|barbed fitting|倒刺接头/], ["check-valve", /check[- ]valve|单向阀/], ["filter", /filter|过滤器/],
   221:     ["plunger-pump", /plunger[- ]pump|柱塞泵/], ["diaphragm-pump", /diaphragm[- ]pump|隔膜泵/], ["pipetting-pump", /pipett|移液泵/], ["syringe-pump", /syringe[- ]pump|注射泵/], ["valveless-pump", /valveless|无阀/], ["tubing", /tubing|管路|软管/],
   222:     ["high-pressure-valve", /high[- ]pressure[- ]valve|高压阀/], ["solenoid-valve", /solenoid[- ]valve|电磁阀/], ["rotary-valve", /rotary[- ]valve|旋转阀|转阀/], ["sampling-probe", /sampling[- ]probe|采样针/], ["piercing-probe", /piercing[- ]probe|穿刺针/], ["wash-probe", /wash[- ]probe|清洗针/], ["mixing-paddle", /mixing[- ]paddle|搅拌桨/], ["air-bubble-detector", /air[- ]bubble|气泡/], ["pressure-sensor", /pressure[- ]sensor|压力传感/],
   223:   ];
   224:   return rules.find(([, pattern]) => pattern.test(text))?.[0] || "product";
   225: }

   227: function inferCategory(kind: ProductKind) {
   228:   if (kind.endsWith("pump")) return "pumps";
   229:   if (["barbed-fitting", "bulkhead-barbed-fitting", "hard-tube-fitting", "thread-to-barbed-fitting", "quick-connect-fitting", "luer-fitting", "female-thread-adapter", "filter", "check-valve"].includes(kind)) return "fittings";
   230:   if (kind === "tubing") return "tubing";

   487: export function getTargetProductMetadataCopy(segments: string[], locale: HardTubeTargetLocale) {
   488:   const pathText = segments.join("/");
   489:   const kind = inferKind({ detailHref: pathText }, pathText);
   490:   const productName = PRODUCT_NAMES[locale][kind];
   491:   const selectionSlugs = new Set([
   492:     "plunger-pumps", "diaphragm-pumps", "pipetting-pumps", "syringe-pumps", "valveless-pumps",
   493:     "hard-tube-fittings", "barbed-fittings", "thread-to-barbed-fittings", "luer-fittings", "quick-connect-fittings", "female-thread-adapters", "bulkhead-barbed-fittings", "filters", "check-valves",
   494:     "high-pressure-valves", "rotary-valves", "solenoid-valves", "piercing-probes", "sampling-probes", "stirring-paddles", "wash-probes",
   495:   ]);
   496:   const isSelectionRoute = segments.length === 1 || (segments.length === 2 && selectionSlugs.has(String(segments.at(-1))));
   497:   if (isSelectionRoute) {
   498:     const categoryKey = segments[0] === "control" ? "control" : segments[0];
   499:     const selectionName = segments.length === 1 ? (CATEGORY_NAMES[locale][categoryKey] || productName) : productName;
   500:     const description = locale === "es"
   501:       ? `Explore la gama FOREACH de ${selectionName}, compare configuraciones, materiales, interfaces y especificaciones para sistemas de manipulación de fluidos de precisión.`

     1: /* =========================================================
     2:    product-image-alt.shared.ts
     3:    FOREACH 官网｜产品图片 SEO ALT 通用工具函数
     4: 
     5:    说明：
     6:    1. 只放通用工具，不放某个产品类型的具体规则
     7:    2. 柱塞泵、隔膜泵、阀、接头后续都可以复用
     8: ========================================================= */
     9: 
    10: export function normalizeText(value: unknown): string {
    11:   return String(value ?? "").trim().replace(/\s+/g, " ");
    12: }
    13: 
    14: export function toUpperCode(value: unknown): string {
    15:   return normalizeText(value).toUpperCase();
    16: }
